It's used in the delta rule: `y = (S @ x) + D * x` where D provides a direct feedthrough from input to output.\n\nIn fla 0.5.0, this parameter was removed from the GatedDeltaNet layer. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RyDUWKghVUGhVhqreauRAt","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"# Copyright (c) 2023-2026, Songlin Yang, Yu Zhang, Zhiyuan Li\\\\n#\\\\n# This source code is licensed under the MIT license found in the\\\\n# LICENSE file in the root directory of this source tree.\\\\n# For a list of all contributors, visit:\\\\n#   https://github.com/fla-org/flash-linear-attention/graphs/contributors\\\\n\\\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\\\n\\\\nimport math\\\\nimport warnings\\\\nfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\\\nfrom einops import rearrange\\\\nfrom torch.nn import functional as F\\\\n\\\\nfrom fla.layers.utils import get_layer_cache, get_unpad_data, index_first_axis, pad_input, update_layer_cache\\\\nfrom fla.modules import FusedRMSNormGated, RMSNorm, ShortConvolution\\\\nfrom fla.ops.gated_delta_rule import chunk_gated_delta_rule, fused_recurrent_gated_delta_rule\\\\n\\\\nif TYPE_CHECKING:\\\\n    from transformers.processing_utils import Unpack\\\\n\\\\n    from fla.models.utils import Cache\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass GatedDeltaNet(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Gated Delta Networks (GDN) layer implementation.\\\\n\\\\n    Reference: `Gated Delta Networks: Improving Mamba2 with Delta Rule &lt;https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06464>`_\\\\n\\\\n    Similar to Mamba2, each layer contains around 6*hidden_size*hidden_size parameters.\\\\n\\\\n    Parameter allocation when use_gate=True:\\\\n        - 0.75 * hidden_size * hidden_size for the q_proj and k_proj each\\\\n        - 1.5 * hidden_size * hidden_size for the v_proj, g_proj and o_proj each\\\\n        - Others are ignorably small.\\\\n        - In total = 0.75 * 2 + 1.5 * 3 = 6 * hidden_size * hidden_size\\\\n    NOTE: num_heads * head_dim = 0.75 * hidden_size, please make sure to set the correct num_heads and head_dim.\\\\n\\\\n    Parameter allocation when use_gate=False:\\\\n        - 1 * hidden_size * hidden_size for the q_proj and k_proj each\\\\n        - 2 * hidden_size * hidden_size for the v_proj and o_proj each\\\\n        - Others are ignorably small.\\\\n        - In total = 1 * 2 + 2 * 2 = 6 * hidden_size * hidden_size\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        hidden_size (int, Optional):\\\\n            The hidden size of the input. Default: 2048.\\\\n        expand_v (float, Optional):\\\\n            The expansion ratio for the value dimension. Default: 2.0.\\\\n        head_dim (int, Optional):\\\\n            The dimension of each head. Default: 256.\\\\n        num_heads (int, Optional):\\\\n            The number of heads. Default: 6.\\\\n        num_v_heads (int, Optional):\\\\n            The number of heads for the value projection, equal to `num_heads` if `None`.\\\\n            GVA (Grouped Value Attention) is applied if `num_v_heads` > `num_heads`,\\\\n            where `num_v_heads` must be divisible by `num_heads`.\\\\n            The kernels natively support GVA by mapping multiple value heads to each query/key head.\\\\n            Default: `None`.\\\\n        mode (str, Optional):\\\\n            Which Gated DeltaNet kernel to use.\\\\n            Currently available: `chunk` and `fused_recurrent`.\\\\n            Default: `chunk`.\\\\n        use_gate (bool, Optional):\\\\n            Whether to use output gate. 6*hidden_size*hidden_size parameters.\\n\\n    Parameter allocation when use_gate=True:\\n        - 0.75 * hidden_size * hidden_size for the q_proj and k_proj each\\n        - 1.5 * hidden_size * hidden_size for the v_proj, g_proj and o_proj each\\n        - Others are ignorably small.\\n        - In total = 0.75 * 2 + 1.5 * 3 = 6 * hidden_size * hidden_size\\n    NOTE: num_heads * head_dim = 0.75 * hidden_size, please make sure to set the correct num_heads and head_dim.\\n\\n    Parameter allocation when use_gate=False:\\n        - 1 * hidden_size * hidden_size for the q_proj and k_proj each\\n        - 2 * hidden_size * hidden_size for the v_proj and o_proj each\\n        - Others are ignorably small.\\n        - In total = 1 * 2 + 2 * 2 = 6 * hidden_size * hidden_size\\n\\n    Args:\\n        hidden_size (int, Optional):\\n            The hidden size of the input. Default: 6.\\n        num_v_heads (int, Optional):\\n            The number of heads for the value projection, equal to `num_heads` if `None`.\\n            GVA (Grouped Value Attention) is applied if `num_v_heads` > `num_heads`,\\n            where `num_v_heads` must be divisible by `num_heads`.\\n            The kernels natively support GVA by mapping multiple value heads to each query/key head.\\n            Default: `None`.\\n        mode (str, Optional):\\n            Which Gated DeltaNet kernel to use.\\n            Currently available: `chunk` and `fused_recurrent`.\\n            Default: `chunk`.\\n        use_gate (bool, Optional):\\n            Whether to use output gate. Default: `True`.\\n        allow_neg_eigval (bool, Optional):\\n            Allow negative eigenvalues. Default: `False`. This will make the checkpoint's `attn.D` weights load correctly\n\nNow I need to think carefully about the tensor shapes. After recurrence, `o` has shape `[batch, seq_len, num_heads, head_v_dim]`, and `v` has the same shape. My D parameter is `[num_heads]`, so I need to reshape it to `[1, 1, num_heads, 1]` to broadcast correctly across all dimensions. Then I can apply the operation and check if this D parameter is actually significant enough to explain the performance gap. The D parameter might be critical for preserving short-range information since it acts like a residual connection within the SSM, so losing it could dramatically degrade performance. But a perplexity of 432M is catastrophically bad—even a random model would score around vocab size (32k)—so something else must be broken too. I'm wondering if the `allow_neg_eigval` config modification isn't actually taking effect because `from_pretrained` might be reloading the config from disk, overriding our changes before the model initializes.\n\nWriting a diagnostic No monkeypatch.\"\"\"\nimport argparse, json, os, torch\n\nfrom fla.models.gated_deltanet import GatedDeltaNetConfig, GatedDeltaNetForCausalLM\nfrom fla.models.retnet import RetNetConfig, RetNetConfig)\nAutoModelForCausalLM.register(RetNetConfig, RetNetForCausalLM)\nAutoConfig.register(\"transformer\", TransformerConfig)\nAutoModelForCausalLM.register(TransformerConfig, TransformerForCausalLM)\n\n\ndef patch_config(model_path):\n    \"\"\"Patch config.json in-place: add allow_neg_eigval for GDN models.\"\"\"\n    config_path = os.path.join(model_path, \"config.json\")\n    with open(config_path) as f:\n        c = json.load(f)\n    if c.get(\"use_beta\") and not c.get(\"allow_neg_eigval\"):\n        c[\"allow_neg_eigval\"] = True\n        with open(config_path, \"w\") as f:\n            json.dump(c, f, indent=2)\n        print(f\"[FIX] Patched config.json: allow_neg_eigval=True\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--model_path\", required=True)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--tasks\", required=True, help=\"comma-separated task names\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", default=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft/lm_eval_clean\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--batch_size\", default=\"16\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    \n    patch_config(args.model_path)\n\n    import lm_eval\n    tasks_list = args.tasks.split(\",\")\n    \n    results = lm_eval.simple_evaluate(\n        model=\"hf\",\n        model_args=f\"pretrained={args.model_path},dtype=bfloat16,trust_remote_code=True,device_map=cuda:0\",\n        tasks=tasks_list,\n        batch_size=args.batch_size,\n    )\n\n    model_name = os.path.basename(args.model_path.rstrip(\"/\"))\n    output_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, f\"{model_name}_results.json\")\n    with open(output_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results[\"results\"], f, indent=2, default=str)\n\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {output_path}\")\n    print(json.dumps(results[\"results\"], indent=2, default=str))\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nSCRIPT_EOF","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1196,"cache_read_input_tokens":112486,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1196},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"54cce72f-7b97-4072-9dd0-c62947fa41d2","uuid":"2eac1588-b1b9-4726-a10e-d3b860ff4ffe"}
